# Plain English words, lowercase, one per line (88k words of three to fifteen letters): the common-words list of the intake.
# A capitalised word whose lowercase form stands here is no candidate on its own (sentence start, heading, ALL CAPS);
# two capitalised words in a row stay a candidate unless the phrase is known, and a first name of rules/first-names.txt
# always stays a candidate. The list is read by awb/words.py (load_plain) and written by hand, not by `awb words update`.
# Source: Moby Word Lists by Grady Ward (Project Gutenberg ebook 3201, https://www.gutenberg.org/ebooks/3201).
#   licence: public domain, "Public Domain material by grant from the author, January, 2001".
#   made from files/common.txt (words in common with two or more published dictionaries) with the inflected forms
#   (-s -es -ed -d -ing -er -ers -ly) that files/crosswd.txt also holds, single lowercase words only, minus every word
#   of files/names.txt, names-f.txt, names-m.txt and places.txt (surnames, given names and places are never plain
#   words here) and minus every first name of rules/first-names.txt.
